\displaystyle:=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})-a^{\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}v\sqrt{\mu}{\mathrm{d}}v,

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{c^{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{2}{3\varepsilon}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot b^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}{\mathrm{d}}v.

Based on the analysis of the macro fluid-type system (
2.20
) and the local conservation
laws (
2.21
), the following nonlinear
estimate deduced by (
2.6
)

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\left\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\zeta\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{v}^{\infty}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}+\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{v}^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{v}^{2}(\nu)},

and the arguments employed in
[
18
]
, we conclude the desired estimate (
2.18
). The details are omitted for
simplicity. Here, we only point out the representation of

\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}_{int}(t)
as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}_{int}(t)=\;&\eta_{0}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\big\langle\nabla_{x}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}b,\,\partial_{x}^{\alpha}a\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x}}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\,g_{b}^{i,j}\partial_{j}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}b_{i}\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 1}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\,g_{c}^{i}\partial_{i}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}c\big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}},\end{split}

(2.22)

where
g_{b}^{i,j}
and
g_{c}^{i}
are linear combinations of

\left[\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{i}\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{i}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{i}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right]

and
\eta_{0}>0
is a small constant. Further, (
2.19
) directly follows from (
2.22
) and the Hölder inequality. This completes the proof.
∎

Next, we perform the

H^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate for the VPB system (
1.3
) with soft potentials
-3<\gamma<0
.

Lemma 2.5
.

Assume that
(f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})
is a solution to the VPB system (
1.3
) defined on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big[C_{0}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\Big(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big)+\sum_{m=1}^{2}C_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\Big]\\
&-C_{0}\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\left(\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\right)\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\Big(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+{\eta}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\end{split}

(2.23)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
, where
C_{0}\gg C_{1}\gg C_{2}>0
are fixed large constants
and
\eta>0
is an enough small constant throughout this section.

Proof.

The proof of (
2.23
) is divided into
three steps as follows.

Step 1.
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of
f^{\varepsilon}
.

Taking the
L^{2}_{x,v}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
inner product of the first equation in (
1.3
) with
f^{\varepsilon}
and then employing (
1.6
), we derive

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq\;&\underbrace{\Big\langle\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\sqrt{\mu}f^{\varepsilon})}{\sqrt{\mu}},f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{1}}+\underbrace{\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{2}}.\end{split}

(2.24)

Using the decomposition (
2.10
) of
f^{\varepsilon}
leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{1}=\;&-\frac{1}{2}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|f^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v\\
=\;&\underbrace{-\frac{1}{2}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v}_{\mathscr{X}_{1,1}}\underbrace{-\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\cdot(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v}_{\mathscr{X}_{1,2}}\\
\;&\underbrace{-\frac{1}{2}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v}_{\mathscr{X}_{1,3}}.\end{split}

Then the Hölder inequality yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{1,1}\leq&\;\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\||v|w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{v}}\left\|w_{\vartheta}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\right\|_{L^{6}_{v}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}}|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\leq&\;\left\||v|w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x,v}}\left\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\\
\leq&\;\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t),\end{split}

(2.25)

where we have utilized the Young inequality to bound

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left\||v|w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{3}_{x,v}}\leq\;&\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big)^{\frac{2}{3}}\Big(\varepsilon^{2}\left\||v|^{3}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{3}{4}}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{3}}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

For the term
\mathscr{X}_{1,2},

the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding inequality imply

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\mathscr{X}_{1,2}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{3}}\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\lesssim\varepsilon\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

For the term
\mathscr{X}_{1,3}
, we derive from (
2.21
) and the Hölder inequality that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{1,3}=\;&-\frac{1}{2}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\Big[\Big(a^{\varepsilon}+b^{\varepsilon}\cdot v+c^{\varepsilon}\frac{|v|^{2}-3}{2}\Big)\sqrt{\mu}\Big]^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v\\
=\;&-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\leq\;&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial_{t}b^{\varepsilon}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathscr{X}_{b^{\varepsilon}}|{\mathrm{d}}x\\
=\;&\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varepsilon\partial_{t}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathscr{X}_{b^{\varepsilon}}|{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\leq\;&\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varepsilon\Big(|\mathscr{X}_{c^{\varepsilon}}|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}|\nabla_{x}\cdot b^{\varepsilon}|\Big)|{b^{\varepsilon}}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\;&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathscr{X}_{b^{\varepsilon}}|{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\leq\;&\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+C\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t),\end{split}

where the fourth equality above is derived from the integrating by parts with respect to
t
.
Consequently, the above estimates on
\mathscr{X}_{1,i}(i=1,2,3)
give us

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{1}&\leq\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\Big(\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.26)

We now deal with the term
\mathscr{X}_{2}
via the collision symmetry of
\Gamma
, (
2.3
), the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding inequality. More precisely,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{2}=&\;\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim&\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{v}^{2}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim&\;\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.27)

Therefore, inserting the bounds (
2.26
) and (
2.27
)
into (
2.24
), we derive

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}-\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}x\Big)+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.28)

Step 2.
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of the pure space derivative
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}

(1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2).

Applying
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}
to (
1.3
) and then taking the
L^{2}_{x,v}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
inner product with
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}
, we obtain by employing (
1.6
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\big(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\big)+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq\;&\underbrace{\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big[\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\sqrt{\mu}f^{\varepsilon})}{\sqrt{\mu}}\Big],\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{3}}+\underbrace{\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{4}}.\end{split}

(2.29)

Recalling the decomposition (
2.10
) and applying a straightforward computation lead to

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{3}=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\frac{v}{2}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{3,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{3,2}}

(2.30)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\frac{v}{2}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{3,3}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\frac{v}{2}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{3,4}}

Then taking the similar way as the estimate of
\mathscr{X}_{1,1}
, we find that

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathscr{X}_{3,1}}\lesssim\;&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\nabla_{x}a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|w\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathscr{X}_{3,3}}\lesssim\;&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\||v|\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{3}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\Big)\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Here we have used a basic inequality

\displaystyle\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}=\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\leq\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\lesssim\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}},

(2.31)

which is derived from (
2.11
) and the Riesz potential theory to the Poisson equation in (
1.3
).
For the term
\mathscr{X}_{3,2}
, we deduce from integrating by parts with respect to
v
,
the Hölder inequality and (
2.31
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{3,2}\;&=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\;&\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{{x}}^{6}L_{v}^{2}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\;&\lesssim\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{{x}}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\;&\lesssim\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Further, by the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding and (
2.31
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathscr{X}_{3,4}}\lesssim\;&\Big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{3}L_{v}^{2}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\Big)\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\Big(\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\Big)\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Hence, substituting the above bounds on
{\mathscr{X}_{3,i}}(i=1,2,3,4)
into (
2.30
) yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathscr{X}_{3}}\lesssim\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.32)

For the term
\mathscr{X}_{4}
,
the collision symmetry of
\Gamma
implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{4}=&\;\underbrace{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big\langle\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{4,1}}\\
&\;+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big\langle\Gamma(\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{4,2}},\end{split}

(2.33)

where the term
{\mathscr{X}_{4,2}}
only exists when
|\alpha|=2
.
Similar to the estimate of
\mathscr{X}_{2}
in (
2.27
), we derive

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{4,1}\lesssim\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Besides, thanks to (
2.3
), the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{3}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{6}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathscr{X}_{4,2}}\lesssim&\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\|\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
&\;+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{6}L_{v}^{2}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{3}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim&\;\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Thus, putting the above estimates on
\mathscr{X}_{4,1}
and
\mathscr{X}_{4,2}

into (
2.33
) leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{4}\lesssim\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.34)

In summary, gathering the bounds (
2.32
) and (
2.34
) into (
2.29
), we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\left(\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.35)

Step 3.
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of the mixed derivative
\partial_{{\beta}}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}

(|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2,1\leq|\beta|\leq 2).

Indeed, thanks to the Maxwellian
structure in the macro part
\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}
, there holds

\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{{\beta}}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

which helps us conclude the
{L^{2}_{x,v}}
-estimate of
w^{|\beta|}\partial_{{\beta}}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}
.
Thus it is adequate to deduce the momentum derivative estimate of

w^{|\beta|}\partial_{{\beta}}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
.
Applying microscopic projection to the first equation in (
1.3
) and using
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu})=0
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\partial_{t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L((\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon})\\
=&\;\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})+[[\mathbf{P},\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}]]f^{\varepsilon}.\end{split}

(2.36)

Here
[[\mathbf{P},\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}]]:=\mathbf{P}\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}-\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}\mathbf{P}
denotes the commutator of two operators and
\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}
is given by

\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}:=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}-\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}+\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}.

Applying
\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}
to (
2.36
) and then taking the
L^{2}_{x,v}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
inner product with
w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
, we obtain by (
2.1
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq&\;\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\|w^{|\beta^{\prime}|}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{C_{\sigma_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
&\;+\underbrace{\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}\Big\langle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}C_{\beta}^{\beta_{1}}\partial_{v}^{\beta_{1}}v\cdot\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{5}}\\
&\;+\underbrace{\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{6}}\\
&\;+\underbrace{\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{7}}\\
&+\underbrace{\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{8}}+\underbrace{\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\big([[\mathbf{P},\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}]]f^{\varepsilon}\big),w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}.}_{\mathscr{X}_{9}}\end{split}

(2.37)

For the term
{\mathscr{X}_{5}}
, the Hölder inequality and the Young inequality lead to

\displaystyle{\mathscr{X}_{5}}\leq\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}+C_{\eta}\|w^{|\beta-\beta_{1}|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-\beta_{1}}\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2},

where we have used

\displaystyle w^{2|\beta|}(v)=w^{|\beta|+\frac{1}{2}}(v)w^{|\beta|-1+\frac{1}{2}}(v)\leq\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}(v)w^{|\beta|}(v)\cdot\nu^{\frac{1}{2}}(v)w^{|\beta-\beta_{1}|}(v).

To estimate the term
\mathscr{X}_{6}
, we integrate by part over
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
and employ the similar procedure as (
2.25
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathscr{X}_{6}}=\;&-\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\nabla_{v}(w^{2|\beta|})}{w^{2|\beta|}}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|w^{|\beta|}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{3}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\Big)\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Similarly, noting that
|\alpha|\leq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{7}=\;&-\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1}\Big\langle\frac{1}{2}\partial^{\beta^{\prime}}_{v}v\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|w^{|\beta|}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}+1}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{3}}\\
\;&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\cdot\|w^{|\beta|}\partial_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\Big)\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Further, with the aid of Lemma
2.2
and following the estimate of
\mathscr{X}_{4}
in (
2.34
), we infer that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{8}\lesssim\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

Thanks to
|\alpha|\leq 1
,
using the integration by parts with respect to
v
when
\partial_{v}^{\beta}

acts on
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
, the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding inequality and the Young inequality, we are able to deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{9}=\;&\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\big([[\mathbf{P},\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}]]f^{\varepsilon}\big),w^{2|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\Big(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\Big)\\
\;&+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{W_{x}^{1,\infty}}\Big(\|w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}}+\|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}}\Big)\\
\;&+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{W_{x}^{1,\infty}}\Big(\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}+\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}}\Big)\\
\lesssim\;&\eta\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{C_{\eta}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\varepsilon\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t),\end{split}

where we have used a fact

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}^{3}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\;&\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}},\end{split}

(2.38)

which is deduced from the Sobolev embedding and the Riesz potential theory to the Poisson equation in (
1.3
).

Thus, substituting the above estimates on
\mathscr{X}_{5}\sim\mathscr{X}_{9}
into (
2.37
), and
then
taking the proper linear combination of the
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate
on
w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}

(|\beta|=m,|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2)
with properly chosen constants
C_{m}>0
for each given
m
,
we get by choosing
{\eta}
sufficiently small that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{m=1}^{2}C_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
1\leq|\beta|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+{\eta}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}\\
&\;+\|\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.39)

In summary, multiplying (
2.28
) and (
2.35
) by a sufficiently large constant
C_{0}
and
combining the result with (
2.39
), we conclude (
2.23
). This completes the proof.
∎

Then we are devoted to the weighted
H_{x,v}^{2}
energy estimate for the VPB system (
1.3
) with soft potentials
-3<\gamma<0
.

Lemma 2.6
.

Let
\ell>0.

Assume that
(f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})
is a solution to the
VPB system (
1.3
) defined on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big(\sum_{m=0}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
0\leq|\alpha|\leq 1,|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\widetilde{C}_{0}\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\Big)\\
\;&\\
&+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
0\leq|\alpha|\leq 1\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{4\varepsilon}\|{w}^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\end{split}

(2.40)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
, where
\widetilde{C}_{0}\gg\widetilde{C}_{1}\gg\widetilde{C}_{2}>0
are fixed large constants.

Proof.

The proof of (
2.40
) is divided into
three steps.

Step 1. The
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of
{w}^{-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}

(0\leq|\alpha|\leq 1)
.

Applying
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}
to (
2.36
),
and then taking the
L^{2}_{x,v}
inner product on the resulting equation with
w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
, we obtain by employing (
2.1
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|w^{-\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w^{-\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}-\frac{C_{\sigma_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq&\;\underbrace{\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{10}}\\
&\;+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{11}}+\underbrace{\left\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\big([[\mathbf{P},\mathscr{A}_{\phi^{\varepsilon}}]]f^{\varepsilon}\big),w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{12}}.\end{split}

(2.41)

Noting that
|\alpha|\leq 1
, by the Hölder
inequality, the Sobolev
embedding inequality and the Young inequality, we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{10}=\;&-\Big\langle\Big(\frac{\nabla_{v}(w^{-2\ell})}{w^{-2\ell}}+\frac{v}{2}\Big)\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\left(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right),w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
=\;&-\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle\left(\frac{\nabla_{v}(w^{-2\ell})}{w^{-2\ell}}+\frac{v}{2}\right)\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle w^{-\ell}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x,v}}\|w^{-\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

By exploiting (
2.3
) in Lemma
2.2
, the term
\mathscr{X}_{11}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{11}=&\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big\langle\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),w^{-2\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim&\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\|w^{-\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
&\;+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\mathbf{P}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{6}L_{v}^{2}}\|w^{-\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{3}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}\|w^{-\ell}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim&\;\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

Observing
|\alpha|\leq 1
and arguing similarly as the estimate of
\mathscr{X}_{9}
,
we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{12}\lesssim{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}+\varepsilon\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

Therefore, plugging the above estimates on
\mathscr{X}_{10}
,
\mathscr{X}_{11}
and
\mathscr{X}_{12}
into (
2.41
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|{w}^{-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|{w}^{-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.42)

Step 2. The
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of
\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}{w}^{-\ell}\nabla^{2}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}
.

Acting
\nabla_{x}^{2}
on the first equation in (
1.3
) and then taking the
L^{2}_{x,v}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
inner product with
\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}
, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|w^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big\langle L(\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}),w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{13}}\\
\leq\;&\underbrace{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big\langle L(\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}),w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{14}}+\underbrace{\Big\langle-v\cdot\nabla_{x}^{2}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu},w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{15}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big[\frac{\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\sqrt{\mu}f^{\varepsilon})}{\sqrt{\mu}}\Big],\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{16}}+\underbrace{\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathscr{X}_{17}}.\end{split}

(2.43)

By the spectral inequality (
2.1
) in Lemma
2.1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{13}\gtrsim\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon}\|w^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}-\frac{C_{\sigma_{0}}}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}.

Observing the Maxwellian
structure (
2.11
) of
\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}
, we also derive

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{14}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}.

For the term
\mathscr{X}_{15}
, the Hölder inequality and (
2.31
) induce

\displaystyle\mathscr{X}_{15}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}a^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}.

Direct calculations yield

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{16}=\;&\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big(\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\varepsilon^{2}w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}-\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big(\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
=\;&-\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Big(\Big(\frac{\nabla_{v}(w^{-2\ell})}{w^{-2\ell}}+\frac{v}{2}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
=\;&-\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 2}\Big\langle\Big(\frac{\nabla_{v}(w^{-2\ell})}{w^{-2\ell}}+\frac{v}{2}\Big)\cdot\nabla_{x}^{2-|\alpha^{\prime}|}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}},\\
\end{split}

which means, by the similar argument as the estimate on the term
\mathscr{X}_{10}
, that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{16}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2-|\alpha^{\prime}|}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle w^{-\ell}w_{\vartheta}^{\frac{1}{4}}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x,v}}\|w^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\big[\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

Based on (
2.10
) and Lemma
2.2
, as in the derivation of
(
2.34
), we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathscr{X}_{17}=\;&\Big\langle\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),w^{-2\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\Big\langle\big|\nabla_{x}^{2}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})\big|,|\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}|\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

Hence, plugging the above bounds on
\mathscr{X}_{13}\sim\mathscr{X}_{17}
into (
2.43
) implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|{w}^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon}\|{w}^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\;\left[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}(t).\end{split}

(2.44)

Step 3. The

L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of the mixed derivative
w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial_{{\beta}}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}

(|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2,1\leq|\beta|\leq 2).

Indeed, the process of the
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate on
w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial_{{\beta}}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
is analogous to the
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate on
w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
under some necessary modifications to match the new velocity weighted
w^{|\beta|-\ell}
. Thus, the method of verifying (
2.39
) applied here yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\sum_{m=1}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
1\leq|\beta|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}\mathscr{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\eta\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
&\;+\|w^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2},\end{split}

(2.45)

where
\widetilde{C}_{1}\gg\widetilde{C}_{2}>0
are fixed large constants.

Consequently,
multiplying (
2.42
) and (
2.44
) by a sufficiently large constant
\widetilde{C}_{0}
and then
combining the resulting inequalities with (
2.45
), we get (
2.40
). This completes the proof of Lemma
2.6
.
∎

With Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.5
and Lemma
2.6
in hand, we are now in the position to give the proof of
Proposition
2.3
.

Proof of Proposition
2.3
.

Choosing
0<\eta\ll\eta_{2}\ll\eta_{1}\ll 1
small enough and taking the linear combination (
2.18
)
\times\eta_{1}+
(
2.23
)
+
(
2.40
)
\times\eta_{2}
induce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)\lesssim\Big([\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathscr{E}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\Big)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)+\Big(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

(2.46)

where
{\mathbf{E}}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t)
comes from the energy given in (
2.22
), (
2.23
) and (
2.40
), as follows

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathbf{E}}^{\mathbf{s}}_{\ell}(t):=\;&C_{0}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\Big(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big)-C_{0}\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}x\\
\;&+\sum_{m=1}^{2}C_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\eta_{1}\varepsilon\mathscr{E}_{int}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\\
\;&+\eta_{2}\sum_{m=0}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
0\leq|\alpha|\leq 1,|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|-\ell}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\widetilde{C}_{0}\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.47)

Thus, (
2.47
) and (
2.19
) mean that
\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)
satisfies (
2.12
). Further, we
utilize (
2.46
) and the
a priori
assumption (
2.14
) to conclude (
2.15
).
This completes the proof of Proposition
2.3
.
∎

2.2.
Weighted
H^{2}_{x,v}
Energy Estimate for Hard Potentials

The main purpose in this subsection is to perform the full
H_{x,v}^{2}
energy estimate coupled with the weighted
H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}
energy estimate, and the high-order
H_{x,v}^{2}
energy estimate with the weighted
H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}
energy estimate for the VPB system (
1.3
) with hard potentials
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
.
To be exact, we introduce the weighted instant full energy
\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
and the weighted instant high-order energy
\mathbf{\widetilde{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\sim\;

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},

(2.48)

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widetilde{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\sim\;

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2}\Big(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big)+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

(2.49)

The corresponding energy dissipation rate is defined as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t):=\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\|\nabla^{2}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}},\end{split}

(2.50)

where the velocity weight
w
is given in (
1.7
).
Then we state the main result of this section.

Proposition 2.7
.

Let
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
.
Suppose that
(f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})
is a solution to the VPB system (
1.3
) defined on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
satisfies the
a priori
assumption

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\leq\delta\quad\text{for}\quad 0\leq t\leq T

(2.51)

for some sufficiently small
\delta>0
, then there exist
\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)

and
\mathbf{\widetilde{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
satisfying
(
2.48
)–(
2.49
) such that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\;

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t),

(2.52)

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathbf{\widetilde{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\;

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

(2.53)

for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

Observing that the technique (
2.25
), which we adopt to overcome the trouble force term
v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}f^{\varepsilon}
for the soft potential case, can be applied to the hard potential case.
Therefore, thanks to the properties (
2.2
) and (
2.4
),
we adopt the methods of justifying Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.5
and Lemma
2.6
to formulate
the corresponding results for the hard potential case under minor modification.
That is, for any
0\leq t\leq T
,
we are able to establish the macroscopic dissipation estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H_{x,v}^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H_{x}^{1}}^{2}+\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathbf{E}_{int}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\left[\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\end{split}

(2.54)

with the temporal interactive functional
\mathbf{E}_{int}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
satisfying

\displaystyle|\mathbf{E}_{int}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)|\lesssim\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}^{2},

(2.55)

the full standard
H^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big[\widetilde{C}_{0}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right)+\sum_{m=1}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\Big]\\
&-\widetilde{C}_{0}\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}x+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\Big(\big[\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\Big)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\left(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+{\eta}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x,v}}^{2}\end{split}

(2.56)

with fixed large constants
\widetilde{C}_{0}\gg\widetilde{C}_{1}\gg\widetilde{C}_{2}>0
,
and the velocity weighted
H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}
energy estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\left[\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\left(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+{\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\;&+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.57)

Consequently, employing the linear combination (
2.54
)
\times\eta_{3}+
(
2.56
)
+
(
2.57
)
\times\eta_{4}
for
0<\eta\ll\eta_{4}\ll\eta_{3}\ll 1
small enough and the
a priori
assumption (
2.51
), we conclude (
2.52
).
Here
{\mathbf{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathbf{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t):=\;&\widetilde{C}_{0}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 2}\Big(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big)-\widetilde{C}_{0}\varepsilon\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})\mathrm{d}x\\
\;&+\sum_{m=1}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\eta_{3}\varepsilon\mathbf{E}_{int}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\eta_{4}\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2},\end{split}

which combines with (
2.55
) meaning that
\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
satisfies (
2.48
).

To prove (
2.53
), we proceed along the same scheme as the proof of (
2.52
).
Comparing the relations of
\mathbf{{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)

with

\mathbf{\widetilde{E}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
given in (
2.48
) and (
2.49
), we find that the main difference lies in the zero-order energy estimate.
In fact, it suffices to replace the zero-order estimate in (
2.56
) by the following estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\left(\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2},\end{split}

(2.58)

which further implies that the
H^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate (
2.56
) is replaced by the following high-order
H^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimate

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big[\widetilde{C}_{0}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right)+\sum_{m=0}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\Big]\\
&+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\end{subarray}}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim&\left(\left[\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}+\varepsilon^{3}\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\mathbf{D}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\eta\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.59)

To prove (
2.58
), multiplying (
2.36
) by
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq&\;\left\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}-\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
&\;-\left\langle\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big\langle\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}.\end{split}

With the aid of the Hölder inequality and the Young inequality, the right-hand first term of the inequality above is bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}+C_{\eta}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}.

Other terms have been handled in (
2.25
) and (
2.27
).
This hence proves (
2.58
).

Thus, using the linear combination (
2.54
)
\times\eta_{5}
+(
2.59
)+(
2.57
)
\times\eta_{6}
for
0<\eta\ll\eta_{6}\ll\eta_{5}\ll 1
small enough and the
a priori
assumption (
2.51
), we derive (
2.53
)
with
{\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}}^{\mathbf{h}}(t):=\;&\widetilde{C}_{0}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right)+\sum_{m=0}^{2}\widetilde{C}_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
|\beta|=m\end{subarray}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\;&+\eta_{5}\varepsilon\mathbf{E}_{int}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)+\eta_{6}\|{w}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.\end{split}

This gathers with (
2.19
) signifying that
\mathbf{E}^{\mathbf{h}}(t)
satisfies (
2.49
).
This completes the proof.
∎

3.
Weighted
W^{{2,\infty}}_{x,v}
-estimate with Time Decay

To close the weighted
H^{2}_{x,v}
energy estimates presented in Proposition
2.3
and Proposition
2.7
, we need to handle the
term involving the
W_{x,v}^{2,\infty}
-estimate of
w_{{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}f^{\varepsilon}
,
where
w_{{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}
is given in (
1.8
). To this end, we introduce a new unknown function

\displaystyle h^{\varepsilon}:=w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}f^{\varepsilon}.

Multiplying (
1.3
) by
w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}
, we get the following system satisfied by
h^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\partial_{t}h^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}-\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)h^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(h^{\varepsilon})\\
=\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\frac{w_{\vartheta}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}h^{\varepsilon},\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}},\end{split}

(3.1)

where

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v):=\;

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}{\nu}(v)+\frac{v}{2}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}+2\widetilde{\vartheta}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\vartheta\sigma}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}|v|^{2},

(3.2)

\displaystyle K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(h^{\varepsilon}):=\;

\displaystyle w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}K\Big(\frac{h^{\varepsilon}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\Big).

(3.3)

In the following, we gather some basic results for the later analysis. Firstly, we present the positive
lower bound estimate for
\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)
, which
is crucial for dealing with the
W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}
-estimate for
h^{\varepsilon}
.

Lemma 3.1
.

Let
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
0<\vartheta\ll 1
,
0<\sigma\leq\frac{1}{4}
and the
a priori
assumption (
1.23
) hold. Then there holds

\begin{split}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\gtrsim\;&\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{2}}{\nu}(v)+\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}.\end{split}

(3.4)

In particular, if
-3<\gamma<0
and
0<\sigma\leq\frac{1}{24}
, then there also holds

\begin{split}\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\gtrsim\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}}(1+t)^{\varrho-1}\qquad\text{with}~~\varrho:=\frac{\sigma\gamma+2}{2-\gamma}\in\big(\frac{3}{8},1\big).\end{split}

(3.5)

Proof.

Via (
3.2
) and the Young inequality, we have, for any small
\eta>0
,

\begin{split}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}\gtrsim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Big({\nu}-\eta\nu(v)\Big)+\Big[\vartheta\sigma-\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{1-\gamma}}(1+t)^{(1+\sigma)}\Big(\frac{1}{2}+2\vartheta\Big)\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{\frac{2-\gamma}{1-\gamma}}\Big]\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}\\
\gtrsim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Big({\nu}-\eta\nu(v)\Big)+\Big[\vartheta\sigma-\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{(1+\sigma)}\Big(\frac{1}{2}+2\vartheta\Big)\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{\frac{5}{4}}\Big]\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}.\end{split}

(3.6)

Here, to deduce the second inequality in (
3.6
), we have used the fact

\displaystyle|v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|\leq\langle v\rangle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq\Big[\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\nu(v)\Big]^{\frac{1}{2-\gamma}}\Big[\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{1-\gamma}}\langle v\rangle^{2}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{\frac{2-\gamma}{1-\gamma}}\Big]^{\frac{1-\gamma}{2-\gamma}}.

Thus, combining (
3.6
) with the
a priori
assumption (
1.23
) and choosing
\eta
and
\delta
small enough, we conclude (
3.4
).

To prove (
3.5
), noticing
-3<\gamma<0
, using (
3.4
) and the Young inequality again, we have

\begin{split}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}\gtrsim\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Big)^{\frac{2}{2-\gamma}}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{2\gamma}{2-\gamma}}\Big[\frac{1}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}\langle v\rangle^{2}\Big]^{\frac{-\gamma}{2-\gamma}}=\;&\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big)^{\frac{4}{2-\gamma}}(1+t)^{\frac{(1+\sigma)\gamma}{2-\gamma}}\\
\gtrsim\;&\varepsilon^{-\frac{4}{5}}(1+t)^{\frac{\sigma\gamma+\gamma}{2-\gamma}}.\end{split}

(3.7)

Observing
0<\sigma\leq\frac{1}{24}
and denoting

\varrho:=\frac{\sigma\gamma+2}{2-\gamma}
, we
conclude (
3.5
). This completes the proof.
∎

Now we turn to the analysis of
K
. From (
1.4
) and changing the variables
v-v_{*}\rightarrow v_{*}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}Kf=&\;\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}q_{0}(\theta)\sqrt{\mu(v_{*})}\left[f(v^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu(v_{*}^{\prime})}+f(v_{*}^{\prime})\sqrt{\mu(v^{\prime})}\right]{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
&\;-\sqrt{\mu(v)}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}q_{0}(\theta)f(v_{*})\sqrt{\mu(v_{*})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
=&\;\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}q_{0}(\theta)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\left[f(v-v_{*}^{\parallel})\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}+f(v-v_{*}^{\perp})\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*}^{\parallel})}\right]{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
&\;-\sqrt{\mu(v)}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v_{*}|^{\gamma}q_{0}(\theta)f(v-v_{*})\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega,\end{split}

where
v_{*}^{\parallel}=(v_{*}\cdot\omega)\omega
and

v_{*}^{\perp}=v_{*}-v_{*}^{\parallel}.

Then by means of (
3.3
) and direct computations, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\partial_{v}^{\beta}\big(K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}f\big)=\sum_{|\beta_{0}|+|\beta_{1}|=|\beta|}\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\big)(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{1}}f),\end{split}

where
\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\big)f

is defined as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\big)f:=&\;\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}q_{0}(\theta)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}{\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v-v_{*}^{\|})}\Big]}{f(v-v_{*}^{\|})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
&\;+\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}q_{0}(\theta)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}{\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*}^{\parallel})}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}\Big]}{f(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
&\;-\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}q_{0}(\theta)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}{\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\sqrt{\mu(v)}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v-v_{*})}\Big]}{f(v-v_{*})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
\equiv&\;\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(v,v_{*})f(v_{*}){\mathrm{d}}v_{*}.\end{split}

In the case of soft potentials, to treat the singularity in
\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}
, we also introduce a smooth cutoff function
0\leq\chi_{m}\leq 1
such that

\chi_{m}(s)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle 1,\quad\quad\text{for}\;|s|\leq m,&\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle 0,\quad\quad\text{for}\;|s|\geq 2m.&\\
\end{array}\right.

Then we use
\chi_{m}
to split
\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}=\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}+\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}

, where

\displaystyle\begin{split}\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}\big)f:=&\;\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}q_{0}(\theta)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\chi_{m}(v_{*}){\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v-v_{*}^{\|})}\Big]}{f(v-v_{*}^{\|})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
&\;+\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}q_{0}(\theta)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\chi_{m}(v_{*}){\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*}^{\parallel})}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}\Big]}{f(v-v_{*}^{\perp})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega\\
&\;-\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}q_{0}(\theta)|v_{*}|^{\gamma}\chi_{m}(v_{*}){\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v)\sqrt{\mu(v-v_{*})}\sqrt{\mu(v)}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v-v_{*})}\Big]}{f(v-v_{*})}{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega.\\
\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}\big)f=\;&\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\big)f-\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta_{0}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}\big)f\equiv\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}l^{c}_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(v,v_{*})f(v_{*}){\mathrm{d}}v_{*}.\end{split}

Then the following result is concerned with the properties of the operator
\partial_{v}^{\beta}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}
.

Lemma 3.2

(
[
32
]
)
.

(1)
Let
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
. For the kernel
l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(v,v_{*})
of
\partial_{v}^{\beta}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}
, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(v,v_{*})e^{\frac{\eta}{8}|v-v_{*}|^{2}}|\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\nu(v)\langle v\rangle^{-2},

(3.8)

where
\eta>0
is a sufficiently small constant.

(2)
Let
-3<\gamma<0
.
For the kernel
l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}(v,v_{*})
of
\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}\big)f
, it satisfies

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}(v,v_{*})e^{\frac{\eta}{8}|v-v_{*}|^{2}}|\mathrm{d}v_{*}\lesssim\nu(v)\langle v\rangle^{-2},

(3.9)

where
\eta>0
is a sufficiently small constant. For the operator
\partial_{v}^{\beta}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}
, there holds

\displaystyle\big(\partial_{v}^{\beta}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}\big)f

\displaystyle\;\lesssim m^{3+\gamma}\mu^{\frac{1}{8}}(v)\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

(3.10)

for any multi-index
\beta
with
|\beta|\geq 0
.

Next, we turn to deducing the
{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
-estimate on the terms related to the nonlinear collision operator
Q
.
Before stating our results, we recall

\displaystyle\partial_{v}^{\beta}\Big[\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}f,\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}g\Big)\Big]=\sum_{|\beta_{0}|+|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=|\beta|}C^{\beta_{0},\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\beta}\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q{{}_{v}^{\beta_{0}}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\partial_{v}^{\beta_{1}}f,\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\partial_{v}^{\beta_{2}}g\Big),

where
Q{{}_{v}^{\beta_{0}}}
is given by

\displaystyle Q{{}_{v}^{\beta_{0}}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}f,\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}g\Big):=\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-v_{*}|^{\gamma}q_{0}(\theta)\partial_{v_{*}}^{\beta_{0}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu(v_{*})}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v_{*})}\Big)\Big[f(v^{\prime})g(v_{*}^{\prime})-f(v)g(v_{*})\Big]{\mathrm{d}}v_{*}{\mathrm{d}}\omega.

Then we have the following result on the nonlinear collision
operator
Q
.

Lemma 3.3
.

Let
-3<\gamma\leq 1
. For any multi-index
\beta
with
|\beta|\geq 0
,
there holds

\begin{split}\Big\|\frac{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q_{v}^{\beta}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}f,\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}g\Big)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\nu(v)\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

Proof.

Notice that

\partial_{v_{*}}^{\beta}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu(v_{*})}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v_{*})}\Big)\leq\frac{{\mu(v_{*})}^{\frac{1}{4}}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(v_{*})}.

Then the proof proceeds in an analogous manner of Lemma 5 in
[
25
]
for the hard potential case
and Lemma 2.3 in
[
32
]
for the soft potential case. The details are omitted here for simplicity.
∎

Finally, we define the characteristics
(X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))
passing through
(t,x,v)
as

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}X(\tau;t,x,v)}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}V(\tau;t,x,v),\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad X(t;t,x,v)=x,&\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}V(\tau;t,x,v)}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}=-\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau,X(\tau;t,x,v)),\quad\quad V(t;t,x,v)=v.&\\
\end{array}\right.

(3.11)

Then under the
a priori
assumption (
1.23
), we study the characteristics estimate
[
25
,
26
]
, which will be
used to control the
W_{x,v}^{2,\infty}
-estimate.

Lemma 3.4
.

Let
-3<\gamma\leq 1
and
T_{0}
be a small fixed number.
Then for
0\leq\tau\leq t\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}
,
there holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{3}}|t-\tau|^{3}\leq\Big|\mathrm{det}\Big(\frac{\partial X(\tau;t,x,v)}{\partial v}\Big)\Big|\leq\frac{2}{\varepsilon^{3}}|t-\tau|^{3}.

Proof.

From (
3.11
), we find

\displaystyle X(\tau;t,x,v)=x-\frac{v}{\varepsilon}(t-\tau)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\tau}^{t}\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{t}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau^{\prime\prime},X(\tau^{\prime\prime};t,x,v)){\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime\prime}{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{\prime}.

(3.12)

Applying
\partial_{v}
to (
3.12
) leads to

\displaystyle\partial_{v}X(\tau;t,x,v)\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}|t-\tau|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}|t-\tau|^{2}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\big\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\big\}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\big\{|\partial_{v}X(\tau;t,x,v)|\big\}.

(3.13)

Thus, we derive from (
1.23
) and (
3.13
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\partial_{v}X(\tau;t,x,v)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}t\lesssim{\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}}T_{0}.

(3.14)

Then consider the Taylor expansion of
\frac{\partial X(\tau;t,x,v)}{\partial v}
in
\tau
around
t

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{\partial X(\tau;t,x,v)}{\partial v}=\;&\frac{\partial X(t;t,x,v)}{\partial v}+\Big.(\tau-t)\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau}\frac{\partial X(\tau;t,x,v)}{\partial v}\Big|_{\tau=t}+\frac{(\tau-t)^{2}}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}^{2}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{2}}\frac{\partial X(\tilde{\tau};t,x,v)}{\partial v}\\
=\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(\tau-t)\mathbb{I}_{3\times 3}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}(\tau-t)\int_{\tau}^{t}\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau,X(\tau;t,x,v))\cdot\partial_{v}X(\tau;t,x,v){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\;&+(\tau-t)\Big[\frac{(\tau-t)}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}^{2}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{2}}\frac{\partial X(\tilde{\tau};t,x,v)}{\partial v}\Big],\end{split}

(3.15)

where
\mathbb{I}_{3\times 3}
is the identity matrix. From (
3.12
), we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\tau}^{t}\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau,X(\tau;t,x,v))\cdot\partial_{v}X(\tau;t,x,v){\mathrm{d}}\tau+\frac{\tau-t}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}^{2}}{{\mathrm{d}}\tau^{2}}\frac{\partial X(\tilde{\tau};t,x,v)}{\partial v}\\
\leq\;&\frac{2}{\varepsilon}|\tau-t|\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\right\}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\partial_{v}X(\tau;t,x,v)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\leq\;&\frac{1}{8\varepsilon},\end{split}

(3.16)

where (
1.23
) and (
3.14
) are utilized. Therefore, we combine (
3.15
) and (
3.16
) to conclude Lemma
3.4
. This completes the proof.
∎

3.1.
Weighted
W^{{2,\infty}}_{x,v}
-estimate with Time Decay for Soft Potentials

In this subsection, we aim to set up the
W^{{2,\infty}}_{x,v}
-estimate of
h^{\varepsilon}
for soft potentials
-3<\gamma<0
.
The main result of this subsection is stated as follows.

Proposition 3.5
.

Let
-3<\gamma<0
.
Suppose that
(f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})
is a solution to the
VPB system (
1.3
) defined on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. And, assume that the crucial a priori assumptions (
1.23
)
and (
1.27
) hold.
Then for any
0\leq t\leq T
, there holds

\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|{h^{\varepsilon}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{5}|\beta|}(1+t)^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{5}{8}|\beta|}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
