\;&+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{5}|\beta|}(1+t)^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{5}{8}|\beta|}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}^{3}{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{5}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.17)

Proof.

To prove Proposition
3.5
, the key point is to obtain the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
-estimate with time decay
for
h^{\varepsilon}
from the zero order to the second order via Duhamel’s principle.

For convenience, we introduce the temporal weighted function

\displaystyle\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}:=(1+t)^{q}h^{\varepsilon}\qquad\text{for }\quad 0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}.

(3.18)

In terms of (
3.1
) and (
3.18
), we have the weighted system

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\partial_{t}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}-\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}(\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})\\
=\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\frac{w_{\vartheta}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon},\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(1+t)^{q}-q{(1+t)}^{q-1}h^{\varepsilon},\end{split}

(3.19)

where the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.19
) only exists when
q>0
.
Then the proof of (
3.17
) is divided into three steps.

Step 1.
L_{x,v}^{\infty}
-estimate of
\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}
.

In fact,
let
T_{0}
be the small fixed number as in Lemma
3.4
. We first consider
\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
on the time interval

0\leq t\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}\ll 1.

Applying Duhamel’s principle to the system (
3.19
),
\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t,x,v)\leq\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(0,X(0),V(0))|+\sum_{i=1}^{5}|I_{i}|,

(3.20)

where
X(s):=X(s;t,x,v)
,
V(s):=V(s;t,x,v)
and

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left[K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\right](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left[K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\right](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\Big[\frac{w_{\vartheta}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon},\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\Big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}(1+t)^{q}\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\Big[q{(1+t)}^{q-1}h^{\varepsilon}\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s.

Based on Lemma
3.1
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nu(V(s))e^{-\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}},

(3.21)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\frac{\langle V(s)\rangle^{2}}{(1+s)^{1+\sigma}}e^{-\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}},

(3.22)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}e^{-\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}},

(3.23)

where
a\equiv\frac{4}{5}
and we have used a basic inequality
derived from (
3.5
)

\displaystyle\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\lesssim\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}}\int_{s}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\varrho-1}\text{d}\tau\Big\}\lesssim\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{2\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}}+\frac{s^{\varrho}}{2\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}}\Big\}.

In the following, we estimate
I_{i}

(1\leq i\leq 5)
in (
3.20
) term by term.
Using (
3.10
) and (
3.21
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{2}|&\lesssim m^{3+\gamma}e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

Recalling the property of
Q
in Lemma
3.3

and the
a priori
assumption (
1.27
), we derive by employing (
3.21
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{3}|\lesssim\;&\varepsilon e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\lesssim\;&\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

By (
3.21
) and the Sobolev embedding inequality, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{4}|\lesssim&\;\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{q}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\right\}\quad\text{with}~\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}\equiv(1+t)^{q}{\phi}^{\varepsilon}.\end{split}

The Young inequality yields

\displaystyle q{(1+t)}^{q-\frac{3}{8}}h^{\varepsilon}\leq q\left[{(1+t)}^{q}|h^{\varepsilon}|\right]^{\frac{8q-3}{8q}}|h^{\varepsilon}|^{\frac{3}{8q}}\leq\eta q|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|+qC_{\eta}|h^{\varepsilon}|,

which combines with (
3.23
) inducing

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{5}|\lesssim&\;\eta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+q\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

To handle the term
I_{1}
, we iterate the bound (
3.20
) of
\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}
into
I_{1}
again to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}|I_{1}|\lesssim\;&e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{2\varepsilon^{a}}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\left(m^{3+\gamma}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}+\eta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\right)e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\right\}+q\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+I_{1}^{r},\end{split}

where
I_{1}^{r}
is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}^{r}:=\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{4}}\int_{0}^{t}&\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(V(\tau))\text{d}\tau\Big\}\int_{0}^{s}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}\widetilde{\nu}(V(\tau^{\prime}))\text{d}\tau^{\prime}\Big\}\\
&\times\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}(V(s),v^{\prime})l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}(V(s_{1}),v^{\prime\prime})\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s_{1},X(s_{1}),V(s_{1}))|\text{d}v^{\prime\prime}\text{d}v^{\prime}\text{d}s_{1}\mathrm{d}s\end{split}

with
X(s_{1}):=X(s_{1};s,X(s),v^{\prime})
and
V(s_{1}):=V(s_{1};s,X(s),v^{\prime})
.
Observing that
l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}(v,v^{\prime})
has a possible integrable
singularity of
\frac{1}{|v-v^{\prime}|},
we choose a smooth function
l_{N}^{c}(v,v^{\prime})
with compact support satisfying

\sup\limits_{|p|\leq 3N}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N}|l_{N}^{c}(p,v^{\prime})-l_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}(p,v^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\leq\frac{1}{N}.

Using the above approximation and the characteristics estimate in Lemma
3.4
, then taking the similar proof as that of the
L^{\infty}_{x,v}
-estimate in
[
42
]
, we establish

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{1}^{r}\leq\;&\Big(\frac{C}{N}+C\eta+o(1)\Big)e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\Big\{e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\}\\
\;&+\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{4}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s-\eta\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{|v^{\prime}|\leq 2N}\int_{|v^{\prime\prime}|\leq 3N}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}{\nu}(V(\tau))\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nu(V(s))\\
&\qquad\qquad\times\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{2\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s_{1}}^{s}{\nu}(V(\tau^{\prime}))\text{d}\tau^{\prime}\Big\}\nu(V(s_{1}))\big|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s_{1},X(s_{1}),v^{\prime\prime})\big|\mathrm{d}v^{\prime\prime}\mathrm{d}v^{\prime}\mathrm{d}s_{1}\mathrm{d}s\\
\leq\;&\left(\frac{C}{N}+C\eta+o(1)\right)e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\Big\{e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big\}+\frac{C_{N,{\eta}}}{\varepsilon^{{1}/{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

Thus, substituting the above estimates on
I_{1}\sim I_{5}
into (
3.20
), we derive
that, for any
0\leq t\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}
, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&e^{\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\right\}+e^{\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\Big(m^{3+\gamma}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}+\eta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}+\frac{1}{N}+\eta+o(1)\Big)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+e^{\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}q\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

Choosing
\frac{1}{N}
,
\eta
,
m
and
\delta
small enough, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\{e^{\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\}\\
\lesssim\;&\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+e^{\frac{({\varepsilon}^{1/2}T_{0})^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\Big[\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\Big]\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{({\varepsilon}^{1/2}T_{0})^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}q\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\left\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.24)

Further, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\;&\frac{1}{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}+q\varepsilon^{\frac{13}{10}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}}\left\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.25)

For
\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}<t\leq T
, there exist
n\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
and
~\tilde{\tau}\in[0,\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0})
such that
t=\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}T_{0}\times n+\tilde{\tau}
. Consequently, using (
3.24
) and (
3.25
) for
q=0
leads to the estimate of
\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}
.
Then taking the resulting estimate into (
3.24
) and (
3.25
), we conclude that for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}
, there holds

\begin{split}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.26)

Step 2.
L_{x,v}^{\infty}
-estimates of
\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}
and
\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}
.

Applying
\nabla_{x}
and
\nabla_{v}
to (
3.19
), respectively and exploiting Duhamel’s principle, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t,x,v)\leq\;&\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(0,X(0),V(0))|+\sum_{i=6}^{11}|{I}_{i}|\\
\;&+\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|\big(\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\big)(s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s\end{split}

(3.27)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t,x,v)\leq\;&\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}|\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(0,X(0),V(0))|+\sum_{i=6}^{11}|I_{i}|\\
\;&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|\big(\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\big)(s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s.\end{split}

(3.28)

Here
{I}_{6}\sim{I}_{11}
are given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}{I}_{6}&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nabla(K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})(s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{7}&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nabla(K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})(s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{8}&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nabla\Big[\frac{w_{\vartheta}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon},\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{9}&=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nabla\Big[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{10}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left[q{(1+t)}^{q-1}\nabla h^{\varepsilon}\right](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{11}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\Big[\nabla\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\Big)\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\end{split}

where
\nabla
represents
\nabla_{x}
if

{I}_{6}\sim{I}_{11}
is in (
3.27
) and
\nabla_{v}
if

{I}_{6}\sim{I}_{11}
in (
3.28
).

Thanks to Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
3.3
, and taking the similar approach adopted in the estimate of
I_{1}\sim I_{5}
, we are able to derive

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{i=6}^{10}|{I}_{i}|\lesssim\;&\left(m^{3+\gamma}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}+\eta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}+\frac{1}{N}+\eta+o(1)\right)e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\nabla\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{2\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\nabla h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\frac{C_{N,{\eta}}}{\varepsilon^{{1}/{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\nabla\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}+q\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.29)

Using the definition (
3.2
) of
\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)
and applying direct computations, we obtain

\displaystyle\nabla\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\Big)\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}|+|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\nabla_{v}{\nu}(v)+\frac{\vartheta\sigma}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}\langle v\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\langle v\rangle\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}{\nu}(v)+\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}},

which means, by (
1.23
), (
3.4
),
(
3.21
) and (
3.22
), that

\displaystyle\begin{split}|{I}_{11}|\lesssim&\;\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.30)

From the
a priori
assumption (
1.23
) and the lower bound estimate
(
3.5
) of
\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}
, the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.27
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}{\mathrm{d}}s\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\lesssim\;&\delta\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}}\int_{s}^{t}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\text{d}\tau\Big\}(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}{\mathrm{d}}s\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\lesssim\;&\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.31)

Moreover,
applying
(
3.5
) and observing the trivial fact that
|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}|\leq(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}|(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}|
, the right-hand last term of (
3.28
) is controlled by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}{\mathrm{d}}s\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}}\int_{s}^{t}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\text{d}\tau\Big\}(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}{\mathrm{d}}s\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.32)

Thus, putting the bounds (
3.29
), (
3.30
) and (
3.31
) into (
3.27
),
and further arguing in the same procedure as the proof of (
3.26
) via (
3.24
)–(
3.25
), we obtain that,
for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}
and for any
0\leq t\leq T
, there holds

\begin{split}\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\lesssim\;&\|\nabla_{x}h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+{\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.33)

Furthermore, based on (
3.28
)–(
3.30
) and (
3.32
) and following the same procedure as the proof of (
3.33
), we easily verify that for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{8}
and for any
0\leq t\leq T
, there holds

\begin{split}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\|\nabla_{v}{h^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{6}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{-\frac{3}{10}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}{{\widetilde{h}}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.34)

Consequently, taking the linear combination of (
3.33
) with
q=\frac{5}{4}
and

\eta\times
(
3.34
)
with
q=\frac{5}{8}
for
\eta
sufficiently small, we conclude by applying (
3.26
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{5}|\beta|}(1+t)^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{5}{8}|\beta|}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\end{split}

(3.35)

holds for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Step 3.
L_{x,v}^{\infty}
-estimates of
\nabla^{2}_{x}h^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}h^{\varepsilon}

and
\nabla^{2}_{v}h^{\varepsilon}
.

Applying
\nabla_{x}^{2}
,
\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}
and
\nabla_{v}^{2}
to (
3.19
) respectively and employing Duhamel’s principle, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t,x,v)\leq\;&\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(0,X(0),V(0))|+\sum_{i={12}}^{17}|{I}_{i}|\\
\;&+\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|\nabla_{x}\left(\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\right)(s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s\\
\;&+\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|\left[\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\right](s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s,\end{split}

(3.36)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t,x,v)\leq\;&\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(0,X(0),V(0))|+\sum_{i={12}}^{17}|{I}_{i}|\\
\;&+\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|[\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}](s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s\\
\;&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|(\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})(s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s,\end{split}

(3.37)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla_{v}^{2}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t,x,v)\leq\;&\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}|\nabla_{v}^{2}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(0,X(0),V(0))|+\sum_{i={12}}^{17}|{I}_{i}|\\
\;&+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left|(\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})(s,X(s),V(s))\right|\text{d}s.\end{split}

(3.38)

Here
{I}_{12}\sim{I}_{17}
are given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}{I}_{12}&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{c}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})(s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{13}&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(K_{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}^{m}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon})(s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{14}&=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big[\frac{w_{\vartheta}}{\sqrt{\mu}}Q\Big(\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon},\frac{\sqrt{\mu}}{w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}}h^{\varepsilon}\Big)\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{15}&=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left[v\cdot\nabla_{x}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}w_{\widetilde{\vartheta}}\right](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{16}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\left[q{(1+t)}^{q-1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}h^{\varepsilon}\right](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\\
{I}_{17}&=-\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\Big[\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\Big)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\Big](s,X(s),V(s))\text{d}s,\end{split}

where
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
represents
\nabla_{x}^{2}
,
\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}
or
\nabla_{v}^{2}

according to the position of
{I}_{12}\sim{I}_{17}
.

Exploiting Lemma
3.2
, Lemma
3.3
and the similar method applied in the estimate of
I_{1}\sim I_{5}
, we establish

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{i={12}}^{16}|{I}_{i}|\lesssim\;&\left(m^{3+\gamma}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}+\eta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}+\frac{1}{N}+\eta+o(1)\right)e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|e^{\frac{s^{\varrho}}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+e^{-\frac{t^{\varrho}}{2\varepsilon^{a}}}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\\
\;&+\frac{C_{N,{\eta}}}{\varepsilon^{{3}/{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}+q\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\left[\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\right],\end{split}

(3.39)

where the last two terms on the right-hand side of (
3.39
) only exist when
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
represents
\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}
or
\nabla_{v}^{2}
.
The
a priori
assumptions (
1.23
) and (
1.27
) imply

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla^{2}_{x}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\Big)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\nabla^{2}_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\;&\lesssim\langle v\rangle|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}||\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|+\langle v\rangle|\nabla_{x}^{3}\phi^{\varepsilon}||\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|\\
\;&\lesssim\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}\left(|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|+|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|\right),\end{split}

(3.40)

which combines with
(
3.22
) inducing

\displaystyle\begin{split}|{I}_{17}|\lesssim&\;\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\quad\text{for }\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}=\nabla_{x}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.41)

Furthermore, direct computations tell us

\displaystyle\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\Big)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}+\nabla_{v}^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}\Big)-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}(v)\nabla_{v}^{2}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\left(|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}|+\langle v\rangle|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}{\nu}(v)+\frac{\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{1+\sigma}}\right)\left(|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|+|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|+|\nabla_{v}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}|\right),

which combines with (
1.23
), (
3.4
), (
3.21
) and
(
3.22
) inferring

\displaystyle\begin{split}|{I}_{17}|\lesssim&\;\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\quad\text{for }\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}=\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}~\text{or}~\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}=\nabla_{v}^{2}.\end{split}

(3.42)

According to (
1.23
), (
1.27
)
and (
3.23
), the last two terms on the right-hand side of (
3.36
) are estimated by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\delta\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}{\mathrm{d}}s\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\;&+\delta\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}{\mathrm{d}}s\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\lesssim\;&\delta\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.43)

Further,
adopting the similar technique
as in the proof of (
3.31
) and (
3.32
), the last two terms on the right-hand side of (
3.37
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.44)

Similarly, the right-hand last term of (
3.38
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.45)

Thus, plugging the bounds (
3.39
), (
3.41
) and (
3.43
) into (
3.36
),
and arguing similarly as the proof of (
3.26
), we find that,
for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}
and for any
0\leq t\leq T
, there holds

\begin{split}\;&\|{\nabla_{x}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{h^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{3}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{{3}/{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\delta\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\delta\varepsilon^{-\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}+\delta\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}.\\
\end{split}

(3.46)

Similarly, collecting the estimates (
3.37
)–(
3.39
), (
3.42
), (
3.44
) and (
3.45
),
we directly establish that
for any
0\leq t\leq T
, there hold

\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\|{\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{h^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{6}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\widetilde{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}+\frac{\varepsilon^{{1}/{5}}}{\varepsilon^{{3}/{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}(1+\delta)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\delta\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{-\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{v}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}~\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad~~~~\text{for each}~~~0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{8}\end{split}

(3.47)

and

\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{5}}\|{\nabla_{v}^{2}{\widetilde{{h}}^{\varepsilon}}}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{5}}\|\nabla_{v}^{2}{h^{\varepsilon}_{0}}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{{\phi}}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}+\frac{\varepsilon^{{2}/{5}}}{\varepsilon^{{3}/{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}^{2}\widetilde{{f}}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{5}}(1+\delta)\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\widetilde{{h}}^{\varepsilon}(s)\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{(1+s)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{{h}}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}~\text{for}~q=0.\end{split}

(3.48)

Thus, for any
0\leq t\leq T
, taking a proper linear combination of (
3.46
), (
3.47
) and (
3.48
), and further gathering (
3.26
) and (
3.35
),
we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{1}{5}|\beta|}(1+t)^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{5}{8}|\beta|}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{21}{10}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}^{3}{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{4}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{5}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{5}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.49)

In summary, combining (
3.26
), (
3.35
) and (
3.49
), we complete the proof of Proposition
3.5
.
∎

3.2.
Weighted
W^{{2,\infty}}_{x,v}
-estimate with Time Decay for Hard Potentials

In this subsection, we turn to establish the
W^{{2,\infty}}_{x,v}
-estimate for
h^{\varepsilon}
with hard potentials
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
.

Proposition 3.6
.

Let
0\leq\gamma\leq 1
. Suppose that
(f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})
is a solution to the
VPB system (
1.3
) defined on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.
And, assume that the crucial bootstrap assumptions (
1.23
)
and (
1.27
) hold.
Then for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}
, there holds

\begin{split}\;&\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+t)^{q}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}(1+t)^{q}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{q}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{q}\|\nabla_{x}^{3}{\phi}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{q}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{q}\|\nabla_{v}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\end{split}

for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

The main idea to deduce Proposition
3.6
is along the same line as Proposition
3.5
for the soft potentials case, but
\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\widetilde{\nu}
for hard potentials has positive bound here, leading to some different treatments in our analysis.
Indeed, thanks to Lemma
3.1
and
\nu(v)\geq\nu_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\nu(V(s))e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}s}{4\varepsilon^{2}}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4\varepsilon^{2}}},

(3.50)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\text{exp}\Big\{-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{s}^{t}\widetilde{\nu}(\tau)\text{d}\tau\Big\}\frac{\langle V(s)\rangle^{2}}{(1+s)^{1+\sigma}}e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}s}{4\varepsilon^{a}}}\mathrm{d}s\lesssim e^{-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{4\varepsilon^{2}}}.

(3.51)

From (
1.23
) and
(
3.50
), we find that the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.27
) and (
3.28
) are bounded by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\delta\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\},\qquad\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\},\end{split}

respectively.
Then exploiting the above two estimates, (
3.50
), (
3.51
), and adopting the similar analysis as the proof of (
3.26
) and (
3.35
), we conclude that, for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}
and for any
0\leq t\leq T
, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{W_{x,v}^{1,\infty}}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\widetilde{\phi}}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x,v}}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.52)

On the other hand, from (
1.23
) and
(
3.50
), we deduce that
the last two terms on the right-hand side of (
3.36
), (
3.37
), and the last term on the right-hand side of (
3.38
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\delta\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}+\delta\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\},\\
\;&\delta\varepsilon^{2}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\}+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\},\\
\;&\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}{\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}}(s)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\right\},\end{split}

respectively. Then employing the estimates above, (
3.51
) and taking the similar process as the proof of (
3.49
),
we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\widetilde{h}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{2}{\widetilde{\phi}}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}^{3}{\widetilde{\phi}}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{x}\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x,v}}\right\}+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\|\nabla_{v}^{2}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\widetilde{f}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right\}\end{split}

(3.53)

for each
0\leq q\leq\frac{5}{4}
and for any
0\leq t\leq T
.
Consequently, gathering (
3.52
) and (
3.53
), we complete the proof of Proposition
3.6
.
∎

Remark 3.7
.

For the hard potential case,
the
a priori
assumption (
1.27
)
can be replaced by a weaker priori assumption: there is
\delta>0
small enough such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{(1+t)^{\frac{5}{8}}\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}\right\}+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{4}}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}\right\}+\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\{\|h^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}}\}\leq\delta.\end{split}

(3.54)

Indeed, the term
D_{5}
defined in (
1.26
) for hard potentials can be controlled directly without the time decay rate
(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{4}}
of
\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}
. On the other hand, the
a priori
assumption (
3.54
) is also enough to control the
velocity growth of
v\cdot\nabla_{x}^{3}\phi^{\varepsilon}h^{\varepsilon}

with the aid of the Young inequality.

In addition, according to (
1.30
), the
a priori
assumption (
3.54
)
can be verified only with the help of the time decay rate
(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{4}}
of

\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}
and
\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}
, rather than the time decay rate of
h^{\varepsilon}
.

4.
Global Solutions and Limit to the Incompressible NSFP System

In this section, our main goal is to establish the global existence and temporal decay estimate for the perturbation VPB system
(
1.3
), and
justify the convergence limit to the incompressible NSFP system (
1.15
) as
\varepsilon\rightarrow 0
.

4.1.
Time Decay Estimate of Energy for Soft Potentials

To close the energy estimate and
W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}
-estimate for soft potentials

-3<\gamma<0
under the
a priori
assumptions (
1.23
), (
1.27
) and (
2.14
), we have to investigate the time decay of
\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)
. For this purpose,
denote

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t):=\;&\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau)+(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{2}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{2}}\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\right\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\Big\{\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 1}\varepsilon^{1+\frac{2}{5}|\beta|}(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{2}-\frac{5}{4}|\beta|}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}^{2}\Big\}\\
\;&+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\Big\{\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=2}\varepsilon^{3+\frac{2}{5}|\beta|}(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{2}-\frac{5}{4}|\beta|}\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}h^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}^{2}\Big\}.\end{split}

(4.1)

The key point is to prove the following main result of this subsection.

Proposition 4.1
.

Let
-3<\gamma<0,

\ell_{0},\ell,\widetilde{\vartheta}
be fixed parameters stated in Theorem
1.2
.
Assume that

\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v=0,

and the
a priori
assumptions (
1.23
), (
1.27
) and (
2.14
) hold true for
\delta>0
small enough.
Then, any strong solution
(f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon})
to
the VPB system (
1.3
) over
0\leq t\leq T
with
0<T\leq\infty
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\lesssim\mathbf{\kappa}_{0}^{2}+\delta\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)+\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{2}(t)

(4.2)

for any
0\leq t\leq T
, where
{\kappa}_{0}
is defined by

\begin{split}\mathbf{\kappa}_{0}:=\;&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|h^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{W^{2,\infty}_{x,v}}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2}\|w^{|\beta|}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 2\\
0\leq|\alpha|\leq 1\end{subarray}}\|w^{|\beta|-(\ell+1)}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(\ell+1)}\nabla_{x}^{2}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\big\|\big(1+|x|\big)f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}+\big\|w^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(4.3)

Indeed, our main idea to deduce Proposition
4.1
is based on the temporal decay estimate on the solution of the corresponding linearized system of VPB system (
1.3
) and Duhamel’s formula. We shall first consider the time decay properties of solutions to the linearized VPB system with a nonhomogeneous microscopic source

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\partial_{t}f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}Lf^{\varepsilon}=h^{\varepsilon},\quad\quad\mathbf{P}h^{\varepsilon}=0,\\[8.53581pt]
\phi^{\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{4\pi|x|}\ast_{x}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}f^{\varepsilon}{\mathrm{d}}v\rightarrow 0\quad\quad\text{as}\;|x|\rightarrow\infty.\end{array}\right.

(4.4)

Given the initial data
f^{\varepsilon}_{0}=f^{\varepsilon}_{0}(x,v)
, we formally define

e^{tB_{\varepsilon}}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}
to be the solution to the linearized homogeneous system (
4.4
) when
h^{\varepsilon}=0
.
Then
we state the following result about the linearized VPB system (
4.4
) for soft potentials
-3<\gamma<0
.

Lemma 4.2
.

Let
-3<\gamma<0,\ell\geq\frac{1}{2}
,
\frac{3}{2}<J<2
and
\ell_{0}>\frac{3}{4}
.
Assume

\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sqrt{\mu}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}v=0

and

\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-({\ell+\ell_{0}})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(\ell+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\||x|a^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{1}_{x}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}<\infty,

Then for any
t\geq 0
, the solution to the system (
4.4
) satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|e^{{tB_{\varepsilon}}}f_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\Delta_{x}^{-1}\mathbf{P}_{0}e^{{tB_{\varepsilon}}}f_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}e^{{tB_{\varepsilon}}}f_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon\|{w}^{-(\ell+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\||x|a_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\|f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|{w}^{-(\ell+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Big)\\
\;&+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{J}\|{w}^{-(\ell+\frac{1}{2})}h^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|{w}^{-(\ell+\ell_{0}+\frac{1}{2})}h^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Big)\\
\;&+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{J}\|{w}^{-(\ell+\frac{1}{2})}h^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}s+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|{w}^{-(\ell+\ell_{0}+\frac{1}{2})}h^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Big),\end{split}

(4.5)

where
\mathbf{P}_{0}
is a projector corresponding to the hyperbolic part of the macro component defined by
\mathbf{P}_{0}f:=\langle f,\,\sqrt{\mu}\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}\sqrt{\mu}
.

Proof.

To make the presentation clearly, the proof of (
4.5
) is divided into three steps.

Step 1. Weighted
L_{v}^{2}
-estimate of
\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}
.
We claim that whenever
\ell>0
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})+\sigma_{0}{D}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\|{w}^{-\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\end{split}

(4.6)

holds for any
t>0
and
k\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Here
{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})

and
{D}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})
are given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})=\;&\|\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{|\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}}{|k|^{2}}+\kappa_{1}\mathfrak{R}{E}_{lin}(\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}(t))+\kappa_{2}\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\chi_{|k|\leq 1}\ \\
\;&+\kappa_{2}\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\chi_{|k|\geq 1},\\
{D}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})=\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\kappa_{1}\frac{|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\hat{f}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\kappa_{1}|\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}+\frac{\kappa_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|{w}^{-\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2},\end{split}

where
0<\kappa_{2}\ll\kappa_{1}\ll 1
are chosen later and
{E}_{lin}(\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}(t))
is defined in (
4.10
).

In fact, the proof of (
4.6
) is similar to the proof of Proposition
2.3
. The difference is that all calculations are made in the Fourier space. Thus, some details in the following proof will be omitted for simplicity. Firstly,
the Fourier transform in
x
from (
4.4
) gives rise to

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\partial_{t}\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}iv\cdot k\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}iv\cdot k\hat{\phi}^{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\mu}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}=\hat{h}^{\varepsilon},\\[5.69054pt]
|k|^{2}\hat{\phi}^{\varepsilon}=\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}.\end{array}\right.

(4.7)

Then taking the
L^{2}_{v}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
complex inner product of the first equation in (
4.7
) with
\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}
and employing (
1.6
), we easily deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big(\|\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{|\hat{a^{\varepsilon}}|^{2}}{|k|^{2}}\Big)+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.8)

Secondly, similar to deriving (
2.20
) and
(
2.21
) from the VPB system (
1.3
), we also derive a macro fluid-type system and the local conservation laws for the linearized system (
4.7
)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle\sqrt{\mu}:&\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}-\frac{3}{2}\hat{c}^{\varepsilon})=\mathfrak{J}_{1},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v_{l}\sqrt{\mu}:&\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{b}_{l}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik_{l}(\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}-\frac{3}{2}\hat{c}^{\varepsilon})+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik_{l}\hat{\phi}^{\varepsilon}=\mathfrak{J}_{2}^{l},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v_{l}^{2}\sqrt{\mu}:&\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\hat{c}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik_{l}\hat{b}_{l}^{\varepsilon}=\mathfrak{J}_{3}^{l},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v_{l}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu}(l\neq j):&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}i(k_{l}\hat{b}_{j}^{\varepsilon}+k_{j}\hat{b}_{l}^{\varepsilon})=\mathfrak{J}_{4}^{l,j},\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle v_{l}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}:&\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik_{l}\hat{c}^{\varepsilon}=\mathfrak{J}_{5}^{l},\end{array}\right.

and

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik\cdot\hat{b}^{\varepsilon}=0,\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{b}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik\hat{\phi}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}ik(\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}+\hat{c}^{\varepsilon})=-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}v\sqrt{\mu}{\mathrm{d}}v+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}v\sqrt{\mu}{\mathrm{d}}v,\\[5.69054pt]
\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{c}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{2}{3\varepsilon}ik\cdot\hat{b}^{\varepsilon}=-\frac{1}{3\varepsilon}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}{\mathrm{d}}v+\frac{1}{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}{\mathrm{d}}v.\end{array}\right.

Here the right-hand terms
\mathfrak{J}_{1},\mathfrak{J}_{2}^{l},\mathfrak{J}_{3}^{l},\mathfrak{J}_{4}^{l,j}
and
\mathfrak{J}_{5}^{l}(l,j=1,2,3)
are all of the form

\displaystyle\begin{split}-\left\langle\partial_{t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f}^{\varepsilon},\zeta\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}}+\left\langle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}+\hat{h}^{\varepsilon},\zeta\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{v}},\end{split}

where
\zeta
represents a (different) linear combination of the basis
[\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{l}\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{l}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{l}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}]
.
Thus, thanks to the above fluid system, as in Lemma
2.4
, we establish the macro dissipation of
{|k|^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}
in the complex space as follows

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathfrak{R}{E}_{lin}(\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}(t))+\frac{|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+|\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2},\end{split}

(4.9)

where
{E}_{lin}(\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}(t))
is given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}{E}_{lin}(\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}(t))=\;&\frac{1}{1+|k|^{2}}\eta_{0}\mathfrak{R}(ik\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}|\hat{b}^{\varepsilon})+\frac{1}{1+|k|^{2}}\sum_{l=1}^{3}\mathfrak{R}\left(ik_{l}\hat{c}^{\varepsilon}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}g_{c}^{l}{\mathrm{d}}v\right)\\
\;&+\frac{1}{1+|k|^{2}}\sum_{l,j=1}^{3}\mathfrak{R}\left(ik_{j}\hat{b}^{\varepsilon}_{l}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}g_{b}^{l,j}{\mathrm{d}}v\right)\end{split}

(4.10)

with
g_{c}^{l}
and
g_{b}^{l,j}
representing some linear combinations of

\left[\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{l}\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{l}v_{j}\sqrt{\mu},\,v_{l}|v|^{2}\sqrt{\mu}\right]
.

Thirdly, applying
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})
to the first equation in (
4.7
) leads to

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}iv\cdot k(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}\;

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left(\mathbf{P}iv\cdot k\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}-iv\cdot k\mathbf{P}\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}\right)+\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}.

Then
taking the
L^{2}_{v}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
complex inner product of the above equation with
\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}
, we are able to deduce the velocity weighted estimate for the pointwise time-frequency variables
(t,k)
over
|k|\leq 1

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|w^{-\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\chi_{|k|\leq 1}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon}\|w^{-\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}\chi_{|k|\leq 1}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\hat{f}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\|{w}^{-\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.11)

On the other hand, applying the
L^{2}_{v}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
complex inner product of (
4.7
) with
\varepsilon w^{-2\ell}\hat{f}^{\varepsilon}
,
we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|w^{-\ell}\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\chi_{|k|\geq 1}+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon}\|w^{-\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}\chi_{|k|\geq 1}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}\|\mathbf{P}\hat{f}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}+\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|{w}^{-\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.12)

Finally, choosing
0<\kappa_{2}\ll\kappa_{1}\ll 1
small enough and taking the linear combination
(
4.8
)
+
(
4.9
)
\times\kappa_{1}+
(
4.11
)
\times\kappa_{2}+

(
4.12
)
\times\kappa_{2}
, we conclude our desired claim (
4.6
).

Step 2. Time frequency Lyapunov inequality in integral form.

Let
0<\widetilde{\eta}\ll 1
and
J>0
be a constant chosen later. Multiplying (
4.6
) by

\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{J}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left[\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{J}{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})\right]+\sigma_{0}\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{J}{D}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})\\
\lesssim\;&\widetilde{\eta}J\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{J-1}\frac{|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})+\varepsilon^{2}\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{J}\|{w}^{-\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.\end{split}

(4.13)

Choosing
0<\kappa_{2}\ll\kappa_{1}\ll 1
small enough, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})&\thicksim\|\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{|\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}}{|k|^{2}}+\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2},\\
&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|{w}^{-\ell}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\frac{|\hat{a}^{\varepsilon}|^{2}}{|k|^{2}}+\|\mathbf{P}\hat{f^{\varepsilon}}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Then similar as

[
15
]
, by splitting the velocity integration into
\left\{w^{-1}(v)\leq 1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right\}

and
\left\{w^{-1}(v)\geq 1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right\}
, we get that for
p>1
and
2\ell_{0}:=J+p-1
, the first term on the right-hand side of (
4.13
) is bounded by

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma_{0}}{2}\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{J}{D}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})+J\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{-p}\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}{E}_{\ell+\ell_{0}}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})\quad\text{for}\quad\ell\geq\frac{1}{2}.

Furthermore, utilizing (
4.6
) implies

\displaystyle{E}_{\ell+\ell_{0}}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})\lesssim{E}_{\ell+\ell_{0}}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}_{0}})+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|{w}^{-\ell-\ell_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

Consequently, noting
p>1
, by the above two estimates and (
4.13
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}{E}_{\ell}(\hat{f^{\varepsilon}})\lesssim\;&\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{-J}\Big({E}_{\ell+\ell_{0}}(\hat{f_{0}^{\varepsilon}})+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|w^{-\ell-\ell_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Big)\\
\;&+\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\Big)^{-J}\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\Big(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}\tau\right)^{J}\|w^{-\ell-\frac{1}{2}}\hat{h}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\end{split}

(4.14)

for any
t>0
and
k\in\mathbb{R}^{3}.

Step 3. Temporal decay of solution to linearized VPB system.

Noting that whenever
J>\frac{3}{2},
we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\int_{|k|\leq 1}\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{-J}{\mathrm{d}}k\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}},\qquad\sup_{|k|\geq 1}\left(1+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}|k|^{2}}{1+|k|^{2}}t\right)^{-J}\lesssim(1+t)^{-J}.\end{split}

Utilizing the above two estimates and the initial condition in Lemma
4.2
, integrating (
4.14
) over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{k}
and then employing a similar process as in
[
15
]
by splitting
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{k}=\{|k|\leq 1\}\cup\{|k|\geq 1\}
, we
derive the desired temporal decay property (
4.5
).
This completes the proof.
∎

Secondly, we establish the temporal decay estimate on
\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}
and
\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}
with the help of Lemma
4.2
.

Lemma 4.3
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left(\mathbf{\kappa}_{0}^{2}+\delta\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)+\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{2}(t)\right)

(4.15)

for any
0\leq t\leq T.

Proof.

By Duhamel’s principle, the solution
f^{\varepsilon}
to
the VPB system (
1.3
) can be expressed as

\displaystyle f^{\varepsilon}=e^{tB_{\varepsilon}}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)B_{\varepsilon}}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(s){\mathrm{d}}s+\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)B_{\varepsilon}}h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(s){\mathrm{d}}s,

(4.16)

where the nonlinear terms
h_{1}^{\varepsilon}
and
h_{2}^{\varepsilon}
are given by

\displaystyle h_{1}^{\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\quad h_{2}^{\varepsilon}=\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}-\frac{1}{2}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}f^{\varepsilon}.

(4.17)

Note that
\mathbf{P}_{0}(h_{1}^{\varepsilon}+h_{2}^{\varepsilon})=0
and
\mathbf{P}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}=0
for all
t\geq 0
.
Then applying Lemma
4.2
to (
4.16
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|w^{-\frac{1}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left(\varepsilon\|{w}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}+\|(1+|x|)f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|{w}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|f^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\right)\\
\;&+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{J}\|{w}^{-1}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|{w}^{-(1+\ell_{0})}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Big)\\
\;&+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{J}\|{w}^{-1}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|{w}^{-(1+\ell_{0})}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\Big)\\
\;&+\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon\|{w}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}+\|h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}^{2}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\;&+\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\Big(\varepsilon\|{w}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Big){\mathrm{d}}\tau,L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}\leq L^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}\end{split}

(4.18)

where
\ell_{0}>\frac{3}{4}
is a constant.

On one hand, applying the similar analysis as that of (6.10) in
[
15
]
, the Hölder inequality,
the definition (
4.1
) of
\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)
and the assumption
\ell\geq\max\{1,-\frac{1}{\gamma}\}+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0}
, we directly infer

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{2}\|w^{-1}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}^{2}=\;&\big\|w^{-1}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{v}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\Big(\|w^{-1}w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|w^{-2}w^{2}\nabla_{v}^{2}f\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\Big)\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-3}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}.\end{split}

Similarly, we are also able to prove

\displaystyle\begin{split}\varepsilon^{2}\|w^{-(1+\ell_{0})}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}^{2}\lesssim\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{v}^{\infty}L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\|w^{-{\ell_{0}}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-3}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}.\end{split}

In terms of the bound (
2.6
), the Hölder inequality,
(
4.1
) and the assumption
\ell\geq\max\{1,-\frac{1}{\gamma}\}+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0}
, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon^{2}\|w^{-1}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}=\big\|w^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})(t)\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}^{2}\|w^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}\|w^{-\frac{1}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\Big\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{5}{2}}\varepsilon\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}^{2}\Big\}(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{2}}\left[\mathbf{D}_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\right]^{\frac{1}{2}}\|f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\|f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}}^{4}\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)(1+t)^{-3}\mathbf{D}_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)(1+t)^{-2}\|f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H_{x}^{2}L_{v}^{2}}^{4}\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)(1+t)^{-3}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+(1+t)^{-3}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}.\end{split}

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|w^{-(1+\ell_{0})}h_{1}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\|w_{\vartheta}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{\infty}}^{2}\|w^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}\|w^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)(1+t)^{-3}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+(1+t)^{-3}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}.\end{split}

On the other hand,
exploiting the expression (
4.17
) of
h_{2}^{\varepsilon}
, the Hölder inequality and the assumption
\ell\geq\max\{1,-\frac{1}{\gamma}\}+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0}
, we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}}+\|h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}\\
\lesssim\;&\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(1+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(-\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\\
\;&+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\left(\left\|w^{-1}w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|w^{-(-\frac{1}{\gamma})}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\lesssim(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

Taking the similar argument as above and the Sobolev embedding inequality, we also deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{{L^{2}_{x,v}}}+\|h_{2}^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left(\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(1+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{w}^{-(-\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}+\ell_{0})}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\\
\;&+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left(\left\|w^{-1}w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|w^{-(-\frac{1}{\gamma})}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\\
\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t).\end{split}

Therefore, substituting the above estimates into (
4.18
) and using
J<2
, we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|w^{-\frac{1}{2}}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\kappa_{0}^{2}+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{J}(1+\tau)^{-3}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\\
\;&+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{J}(1+\tau)^{-3}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1+\tau)^{-3}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\kappa_{0}^{2}+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\delta\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)\right]^{2},\end{split}

(4.19)

which leads to (
4.15
) immediately. This completes the proof.
∎

Finally, with Lemma
4.3
in hand, we give the proof of Proposition
4.1
.

Proof of Proposition
4.1
.

To make the presentation clear, we divide the proof of Proposition
4.1
into three steps.

Step 1. Optimal time decay estimate on
\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)
.

From Proposition
2.3
and the
a priori
assumption (
1.27
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\lesssim 0.

(4.20)

Take
0<p<\frac{1}{2}
small enough. Multiplying the uniform energy inequality (
4.20
) by
(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}+p}
, and further taking the time integration over
[0,t]
, we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}+p}\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}+p}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\big(\frac{3}{2}+p\big)\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}\left(\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}\mathbf{D}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau.\end{split}

(4.21)

Here we have used the inequality

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+\mathbf{D}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)\quad\;\text{for any}\;\ell>0,

(4.22)

which is derived from (
2.12
) and
(
2.13
).

Similarly, we derive from (
4.20
) with
\ell
replaced by
\ell+\frac{1}{2}
and further multiply it by
(1+t)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&(1+t)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}\mathbf{E}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}\mathbf{D}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\big(\frac{1}{2}+p\big)\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{p-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{E}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell+\frac{1}{2}}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{D}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau,\end{split}

(4.23)

where the last inequality above used a fact

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\int^{t}_{0}\mathbf{D}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0),

which is directly derived from the time integration of (
4.20
) with
\ell
replaced by
\ell+1
.
Further, substituting the estimate (
4.23
) into (
4.21
) and applying Lemma
4.3
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}+p}\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}+p}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}\left(\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\tau\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\left(\mathbf{\kappa}_{0}^{2}+\delta\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)+\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{2}(t)\right)\\
\lesssim\;&\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)+(1+t)^{p}\left(\mathbf{\kappa}_{0}^{2}+\delta\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)+\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{2}(t)\right),\end{split}

(4.24)

where we have used a basic inequality

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{1}{2}+p}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim(1+t)^{p}.

On the other hand, in terms of
\Delta_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}_{0}(x)=a^{\varepsilon}_{0}(x)
, the interpolation inequality and the Young inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\;

\displaystyle\lesssim\|a_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6/5}_{x}}\lesssim\|a_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|a_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\lesssim\|f_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|f_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\phi^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\;

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}^{2}\Delta^{-1}a_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\|a_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\|f_{0}^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}.

These mean that
\|\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}_{0}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}
can be removed from
\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)
by the definition of (
2.12
) and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{\ell+1}^{\mathbf{s}}(0)\lesssim\kappa_{0}^{2}

(4.25)

holds true with
\kappa_{0}
given by (
4.3
).
Therefore, combining (
4.24
) with (
4.25
), we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{E}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau)\right\}+(1+t)^{-p}\int_{0}^{t}(1+\tau)^{\frac{3}{2}+p}\mathbf{D}_{\ell}^{\mathbf{s}}(\tau){\mathrm{d}}\tau\lesssim\mathbf{\kappa}_{0}^{2}+\delta\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)+\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{2}(t).\end{split}

(4.26)

Step 2. Optimal time decay estimate on
\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}(t)\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}
.

To this end, we
first perform the energy estimate without time decay factor.
In fact,
employing similar derivation in (
2.29
) leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq&\underbrace{-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\left\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{J_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\left\langle\frac{v}{2}\cdot\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\nabla_{x}\phi^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{J_{2}}\\
&+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon})+\Gamma(\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{J_{3}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\langle\Gamma(\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}),\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{J_{4}},\end{split}

(4.27)

where the last term
J_{4}
only exists when
|\alpha|=2
.

Applying the same estimate of

\mathscr{X}_{3}
in (
2.32
),
the Young inequality and the definition (
4.1
) of
\mathbf{X}_{\ell}(t)
, we derive

\displaystyle\begin{split}J_{1}\lesssim\;&\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\Big(\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|w\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\Big)\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}\\
\;&+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\alpha^{\prime}|<|\alpha|\end{subarray}}\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{{x}}^{6}L_{v}^{2}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\|a^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}^{2}\left(\|w\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H_{x}^{1}L_{v}^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{6}\|w_{\vartheta}\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{W^{1,\infty}_{x,v}}^{2}\right)\\
